# Agents · P · part 33

902 entries, product-scout to profitability. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **product-scout**: Finds and compares building and interior products (floors, tiles, wallpaper, paint, radiators, fixtures) in the owner's local shops, normalises prices, flags doubtful units, and recommends options by price per m2 or per kW and fit with the plan. Use when the owner asks what to buy or where. ([gitkvash/home-design-toolkit](https://github.com/gitkvash/home-design-toolkit/blob/HEAD/agents/product-scout.md))
- **product-scout**: Use this agent during FRAME to research product context — user needs, prior art, competing approaches, and the shape of the problem the cycle is trying to solve. Spawn it as one member of the FRAME scout team when the dominant uncertainty is "what should we build and why". Example — lead: "We are framing a cycle for offline sync. Send product-scout to survey existing sync docs, user reports, and prior product decisions in the repo, and report the product-side unknowns. ([hlbathien/greenfield-foundry](https://github.com/hlbathien/greenfield-foundry/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/product-scout.md))
- **product-scribe**: The writing arm of /learn-app (the Workflow engine). - **profiles**: Read the user's **Agent Profiles** and start one — optionally with an opening prompt. A profile is the user's own named recipe for launching a coding agent (a label, a command line like `claude-work`, and an optional config directory for running a second account), defined on **Settings → Agents → Profiles**. ([silo-code/silo](https://github.com/silo-code/silo/blob/HEAD/apps/docs/api/agents/profiles.md) · ★62)
- **profiles**: Agents are specialized AI workers with their own identity, system prompt, tool access, skills, model overrides, and connection mode. The UI writes the same files described here, so anything created in **Settings &gt; Agents** can also be reviewed and versioned on disk. ([aj47/dotagents-mono](https://github.com/aj47/dotagents-mono/blob/HEAD/docs-site/docs/agents/profiles.md) · ★17)
- **profiles**: Every agent that touches a structured-artifact store maps to **exactly one** profile. The profile fixes the model default and the set of tools the agent must not call. Mixing profiles in one agent means it can no longer be safely composed in a pipeline — refuse, and split it into two agents. ([ForgePlan/marketplace](https://github.com/ForgePlan/marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cc-best/skills/cc-best/sections/agents/profiles.md) · ★4)
